# VWAP + RSI Trend — QQQ

From [[VWAP Strategy Master Reference]] + [[Investing - Short Term (Day Trading)]].

> "VWAP is not a signal — it is a context engine." — VWAP Master Reference

Dual confirmation: buy QQQ only when both VWAP AND momentum (RSI > 50) align.  
Exit when RSI gets overbought (>75) — momentum exhaustion signal.

## Strategy Logic

- **VWAP filter:** price above 20-day rolling VWAP = bullish regime
- **RSI entry:** must be above 50 on crossover day = momentum confirmed
- **RSI exit:** above 75 = overbought, take profit
- **Stop:** 4% — slightly wider than SPY given QQQ's tech volatility

## Notes

Covers the 2022 bear market — interesting to see how VWAP + RSI handles drawdowns.
From the real-data simulations: trending sessions dominate (59.5%) in the 60-day window.
